Implement the following plan:

# 순수 피지컬 모드 추가 계획

## Context
난이도 선택 화면에서 EASY를 제거하고, 그 자리에 **순수 피지컬 모드** 버튼을 추가한다.
순수 피지컬 모드는 선택된 캐릭터의 스프라이트는 유지하되, 특수 능력은 모두 제거하여
모든 플레이어가 동일 조건에서 실력으로 경쟁하는 모드다.
내부 난이도 파라미터는 NORMAL 설정을 그대로 사용한다.

---

## 변경 파일 (3개)

### 1. `src/types/GameMode.ts`
- `DIFFICULTIES` 배열에서 EASY 항목 제거
- `Difficulty` const 객체에서 `EASY` 키 제거
- `DifficultyConfig` 인터페이스는 그대로 유지

### 2. `src/scenes/DifficultySelectScene.ts`
현재 2×2 그리드: EASY / NORMAL / HARD / EXTREME
→ 변경 후 2×2 그리드: **NORMAL / HARD / EXTREME / 순수 피지컬**

**순수 피지컬 버튼 추가**
- `create()` 마지막에 `createPureButton()` 메서드 호출 (4번째 셀 위치: col=1, row=1 → x=300, y=390)
- 색상: `0xeeeeee` (밝은 회색), stroke: `0x888888`
- 이모지: ⚡, 제목: "순수 피지컬", 설명: "능력 없음 · 순수 실력"
- 클릭 시: `scene.start('GameScene', { gameMode: this.gameMode, difficulty: 'NORMAL', purePhysical: true })`

```typescript
private createPureButton(x: number, y: number) { ... }
```

### 3. `src/scenes/GameScene.ts`
- `init()` data 타입에 `purePhysical?: boolean` 추가
- `init()` 내에서 `purePhysical` 플래그를 인스턴스 변수로 저장
- `this.ability = getCharacterAbility(...)` 호출 직후, `purePhysical` 이 true 이면
  `this.ability = new BaseAbility()` 로 덮어쓰기
- `BaseAbility` import 추가 (`src/abilities/BaseAbility.ts`)

```typescript
// init() 안
this.ability = getCharacterAbility(this.selectedCharId, awakeLevel);
if (data.purePhysical) this.ability = new BaseAbility();
```

---

## 재사용할 기존 코드
- `BaseAbility` (`src/abilities/BaseAbility.ts`) — chibi 기본 능력 클래스, 모든 메서드가 no-op
- `createDifficultyButton()` 패턴 참고해서 `createPureButton()` 작성
- 그리드 좌표: `startX=100, startY=210, spacingX=200, spacingY=180` → 4번째 셀 (col=1, row=1) = x=300, y=390

---

## 검증
1. `npx tsc --noEmit` — 타입 에러 없는지 확인
2. EASY 버튼이 사라지고 2×2 그리드가 깔끔하게 채워지는지 확인
3. 순수 피지컬 모드 진입 시 SR/UR 캐릭터 선택 상태에서도 능력 발동 없는지 플레이로 확인
4. 기존 NORMAL/HARD/EXTREME 버튼 동작 정상인지 확인

   138→  private createDifficultyButton(difficultyConfig: DifficultyConfig, x: number, y: number) {
   139→    // 그림자 효과
   140→    const shadow = this.add.rectangle(x + 3, y + 3, 170, 150, 0x000000, 0.5);
   141→
   142→    // 카드 배경
   143→    const button = this.add.rectangle(x, y, 170, 150, difficultyConfig.color, 1);
   144→    button.setStrokeStyle(4, 0x000000);
   145→
   146→    // 내부 테두리 (깊이감)
   147→    const innerBorder = this.add.rectangle(x, y, 160, 140, difficultyConfig.color, 0)
   148→      .setStrokeStyle(2, this.darkenColor(difficultyConfig.color));
   149→
   150→    // 난이도 이모지
   151→    const emoji = this.getDifficultyEmoji(difficultyConfig.difficulty);
   152→    const emojiText = this.add.text(x, y - 48, emoji, {
   153→      fontSize: '32px'
   154→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   155→
   156→    // 난이도 이름
   157→    const title = this.add.text(x, y - 15, difficultyConfig.name, {
   158→      fontSize: '20px',
   159→      color: '#000',
   160→      fontStyle: 'bold',
   161→      stroke: '#fff',
   162→      strokeThickness: 2
   163→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   164→
   165→    // 설명
   166→    const description = this.add.text(x, y + 10, difficultyConfig.description, {
   167→      fontSize: '11px',
   168→      color: '#222',
   169→      align: 'center'
   170→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   171→
   172→    // 정보 박스
   173→    const infoBg = this.add.rectangle(x, y + 42, 150, 38, 0xffffff, 0.8);
   174→
   175→    const poopInfo = this.add.text(x, y + 34, `💩 ${difficultyConfig.poopCount}개`, {
   176→      fontSize: '11px',
   177→      color: '#000',
   178→      fontStyle: 'bold'
   179→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   180→
   181→    const speedInfo = this.add.text(x, y + 50, `⚡ ${this.getSpeedText(difficultyConfig.baseSpeed)}`, {
   182→      fontSize: '11px',
   183→      color: '#000',
   184→      fontStyle: 'bold'
   185→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   186→
   187→    button.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   188→    const elements = [button, innerBorder, emojiText, title, description, infoBg, poopInfo, speedInfo];
   189→    const originalColor = difficultyConfig.color;
   190→
   191→    elements.forEach(element => {
   192→      if (element !== button) {
   193→        element.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   194→      }
   195→
   196→      element.on('pointerover', () => {
   197→        button.setFillStyle(this.lightenColor(originalColor));
   198→        button.setScale(1.08);
   199→        shadow.setScale(1.08);
   200→        innerBorder.setScale(1.08);
   201→        emojiText.setScale(1.1);
   202→        title.setScale(1.05);
   203→        description.setScale(1.05);
   204→        infoBg.setScale(1.08);
   205→        poopInfo.setScale(1.05);
   206→        speedInfo.setScale(1.05);
   207→      });
   208→
   209→      element.on('pointerout', () => {
   210→        button.setFillStyle(originalColor);
   211→        button.setScale(1);
   212→        shadow.setScale(1);
   213→        innerBorder.setScale(1);
   214→        emojiText.setScale(1);
   215→        title.setScale(1);
   216→        description.setScale(1);
   217→        infoBg.setScale(1);
   218→        poopInfo.setScale(1);
   219→        speedInfo.setScale(1);
   220→      });
   221→
   222→      element.on('pointerdown', () => {
   223→        this.startGame(difficultyConfig.difficulty);
   224→      });
   225→    });
   226→  }
   227→
   228→  private getDifficultyEmoji(difficulty: string): string {
   229→    switch (difficulty) {
   230→      case 'easy': return '😊';
   231→      case 'normal': return '😐';
   232→      case 'hard': return '😰';
   233→      case 'extreme': return '💀';
   234→      default: return '🎮';
   235→    }
   236→  }
   237→
   238→  private getSpeedText(baseSpeed: number): string {
   239→    if (baseSpeed <= 150) return '느림';
   240→    if (baseSpeed <= 175) return '보통';
   241→    if (baseSpeed <= 200) return '빠름';
   242→    return '매우 빠름';
   243→  }
   244→
   245→  private lightenColor(color: number): number {
   246→    // 색상을 밝게 만들기
   247→    const r = Math.min(255, ((color >> 16) & 0xFF) + 30);
   248→    const g = Math.min(255, ((color >> 8) & 0xFF) + 30);
   249→    const b = Math.min(255, (color & 0xFF) + 30);
   250→    return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
   251→  }
   252→
   253→  private darkenColor(color: number): number {
   254→    // 색상을 어둡게 만들기
   255→    const r = Math.max(0, ((color >> 16) & 0xFF) - 40);
   256→    const g = Math.max(0, ((color >> 8) & 0xFF) - 40);
   257→    const b = Math.max(0, (color & 0xFF) - 40);
   258→    return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
   259→  }
   260→
   261→  private startGame(difficulty: Difficulty) {
   262→    this.scene.start('GameScene', {
   263→      gameMode: this.gameMode,
   264→      difficulty: difficulty
   265→    });
   266→  }
   267→}
   268→

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **Strategy 패턴 활용**: `BaseAbility`가 이미 모든 메서드를 no-op으로 구현하므로, `purePhysical` 모드는 단순히 `this.ability`를 `new BaseAbility()`로 교체하면 능력이 완전히 차단됨
- **그리드 좌표**: `startX=100, spacingX=200`이므로 4번째 셀(col=1, row=1)은 x=300, y=390

> AGENT

타입 에러 없이 통과했습니다. 최종 결과를 요약합니다.

---

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **BaseAbility 교체 트릭**: Strategy 패턴의 장점 — `if (purePhysical) this.ability = new BaseAbility()` 한 줄로 모든 캐릭터 능력을 완전히 비활성화. 각 능력 메서드를 개별 차단할 필요가 없음
- **EASY 제거 연쇄 효과**: `Difficulty.EASY`를 삭제하자 GameScene(배경 선택), LeaderboardScene(버튼 배치) 두 곳에서 참조 오류가 발생 — TypeScript 엄격 모드 덕분에 누락 없이 모두 감지됨
- **LeaderboardScene 레이아웃**: 4개→3개 버튼으로 줄면서 `startX` 공식과 인덱스 역산 공식 모두 수정 필요. 공식은 `startX = 200 - spacing * (N-1)/2`를 기억하면 됨
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

**구현 완료 내용:**

| 파일 | 변경 사항 |
|------|-----------|
| `src/types/GameMode.ts` | `Difficulty.EASY` 및 DIFFICULTIES의 EASY 항목 제거 |
| `src/scenes/DifficultySelectScene.ts` | `createPureButton()` 추가 — 4번째 셀(x=300, y=390) |
| `src/scenes/GameScene.ts` | `purePhysical` 플래그 처리, `BaseAbility` import 추가, EASY 배경 참조 제거 |
| `src/scenes/LeaderboardScene.ts` | EASY 버튼 제거, 3개 버튼 중앙 정렬 수정 |

- 순수 피지컬 버튼: ⚡ 아이콘, 밝은 회색(#eeeeee), "능력 없음 · 순수 실력" 설명
- 클릭 시 `purePhysical: true`로 GameScene 진입 → SR/UR 캐릭터여도 `BaseAbility()` (no-op) 사용

> DEVELOPER

난이도 선택에서 순수 피지컬이 아니라 PHYSICAL 로 난이도명 바꿔주고 설명에 능력이 없는건 맞는데 extreme 기준으로 해줘

> AGENT

점수 저장(로컬 최고 점수 + Firebase 리더보드)이 모두 `this.difficulty`를 키로 사용하고 있습니다. PHYSICAL 전용 키가 필요합니다.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- 현재 `this.difficulty`가 두 가지 역할을 겸함: ① 게임플레이 파라미터 선택 ② 점수 저장 키. PHYSICAL은 게임플레이는 EXTREME이지만 점수 키만 분리해야 하므로 `scoreDifficulty` 필드를 별도로 두는 게 가장 깔끔함
- `Difficulty` 타입에 `'physical'` 추가 → 타입 안전하게 localStorage 키(`highScore_physical`)와 Firebase 경로(`/leaderboard/physical/`)가 자동으로 분리됨
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

**추가된 흐름:**
```
PHYSICAL 버튼 클릭
  → difficulty: 'extreme' (게임플레이 파라미터)
  → purePhysical: true
  → scoreDifficulty = 'physical' (점수 저장 키)
  → getHighScore('physical') — EXTREME과 별도 로컬 기록
  → submitScore(..., 'physical') — Firebase /leaderboard/physical/ 별도 경로
  → lastPlayedDifficulty = 'physical' — 리더보드에서 PHYSICAL 탭으로 복귀
```

**리더보드 탭:** NORMAL / HARD / EXTREME / physical (4개, 간격 축소해서 배치)

     1→import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
     2→
     3→const corsHeaders = {
     4→  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
     5→  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
     6→};
     7→
     8→// 난이도별 물리적 최대 점수 (2시간 연속 플레이 기준)
     9→const SCORE_CAPS: Record<string, number> = {
    10→  easy: 10000,
    11→  normal: 15000,
    12→  hard: 20000,
    13→  extreme: 30000,
    14→};
    15→
    16→Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
    17→  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    18→    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    19→  }
    20→
    21→  try {
    22→    const { score, difficulty, userName, sessionId, verification, characterType } = await req.json();
    23→
    24→    // 기본 입력 검증
    25→    if (typeof score !== 'number' || score < 0) {
    26→      return new Response(
    27→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid score' }),
    28→        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    29→      );
    30→    }
    31→
    32→    const validDifficulties = ['easy', 'normal', 'hard', 'extreme'];
    33→    if (!validDifficulties.includes(difficulty)) {
    34→      return new Response(
    35→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid difficulty' }),
    36→        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    37→      );
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    if (!userName || !/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(userName)) {
    41→      return new Response(
    42→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid userName: must be 3 uppercase letters' }),
    43→        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    44→      );
    45→    }
    46→
    47→    // 하드 캡: 물리적으로 불가능한 점수 차단
    48→    if (score > (SCORE_CAPS[difficulty] ?? 10000)) {
    49→      console.warn('Score exceeds hard cap', { score, difficulty });
    50→      return new Response(
    51→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Score exceeds maximum possible value' }),
    52→        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    53→      );
    54→    }
    55→
    56→    // JWT 인증
    57→    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    58→    if (!authHeader) {
    59→      return new Response(
    60→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing authorization header' }),
    61→        { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    62→      );
    63→    }
    64→
    65→    const supabaseUser = createClient(
    66→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
    67→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '',
    68→      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }
    69→    );
    70→
    71→    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabaseUser.auth.getUser();
    72→    if (authError || !user) {
    73→      return new Response(
    74→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }),
    75→        { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    76→      );
    77→    }
    78→
    79→    const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
    80→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
    81→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? ''
    82→    );
    83→
    84→    // ── 세션 검증: 서버가 기록한 start_time 기준으로 경과 시간 확인 ──────────
    85→    // UPDATE WHERE used=false RETURNING ... 패턴으로 원자적 검증 + 소비 처리
    86→    // SELECT 후 UPDATE 방식은 동시 요청 시 race condition 가능 → 이 방식으로 방지
    87→    if (sessionId) {
    88→      const { data: session } = await supabaseAdmin
    89→        .from('game_sessions')
    90→        .update({ used: true })
    91→        .eq('id', sessionId)
    92→        .eq('used', false)
    93→        .eq('user_id', user.id)
    94→        .eq('difficulty', difficulty)
    95→        .select('start_time')
    96→        .single();
    97→
    98→      // null → 세션 없음 / 이미 사용됨 / 소유자 불일치 / 난이도 불일치 중 하나
    99→      if (!session) {
   100→        return new Response(
   101→          JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid or already used session' }),
   102→          { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   103→        );
   104→      }
   105→
   106→      // 서버 시각 기준 경과 시간으로 점수 타당성 검증
   107→      const elapsedMs = Date.now() - new Date(session.start_time).getTime();
   108→      const elapsedSec = elapsedMs / 1000;
   109→
   110→      // 세션 만료 체크 (2시간 초과)
   111→      if (elapsedMs > 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
   112→        return new Response(
   113→          JSON.stringify({ error: 'Session expired' }),
   114→          { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   115→        );
   116→      }
   117→
   118→      // 핵심 검증: 이 점수가 나오려면 최소 N초는 걸렸어야 함
   119→      // 1점 = 100ms → score점 = score * 0.1초, 50% 여유 적용
   120→      const minRequiredSec = score * 0.1 * 0.5;
   121→      if (elapsedSec < minRequiredSec) {
   122→        console.warn('Session time gate failed', { score, elapsedSec, minRequiredSec });
   123→        return new Response(
   124→          JSON.stringify({ error: 'Score not achievable in elapsed time' }),
   125→          { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   126→        );
   127→      }
   128→    } else if (verification) {
   129→      // 세션 없이 구 방식 verification만 있는 경우 (하위 호환)
   130→      const {
   131→        gameStartTime,
   132→        gameEndTime,
   133→        goldCollected = 0,
   134→        diamondCollected = 0,
   135→        topazCollected = 0,
   136→        rainbowCollected = 0,
   137→      } = verification;
   138→
   139→      const playDuration = gameEndTime - gameStartTime;
   140→      const timeScore = Math.floor(playDuration / 100);
   141→      const bonusScore =
   142→        goldCollected * 20 +
   143→        diamondCollected * 40 +
   144→        topazCollected * 80 +
   145→        rainbowCollected * 90;
   146→      const expectedScore = timeScore + bonusScore;
   147→      const tolerance = Math.max(expectedScore * 0.2, 10);
   148→
   149→      if (score > expectedScore + tolerance) {
   150→        console.warn('Score verification failed', { score, expectedScore, playDuration });
   151→        return new Response(
   152→          JSON.stringify({ error: 'Score verification failed' }),
   153→          { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   154→        );
   155→      }
   156→    }
   157→
   158→    // profiles 테이블에 이니셜 업데이트
   159→    await supabaseAdmin
   160→      .from('profiles')
   161→      .update({ initials: userName })
   162→      .eq('id', user.id);
   163→
   164→    // 기존 점수 조회
   165→    const { data: existing } = await supabaseAdmin
   166→      .from('leaderboard')
   167→      .select('score')
   168→      .eq('user_id', user.id)
   169→      .eq('difficulty', difficulty)
   170→      .single();
   171→
   172→    const previousScore = existing?.score ?? null;
   173→    const isNewRecord = previousScore === null || score > previousScore;
   174→
   175→    // 최고 점수만 저장 (upsert)
   176→    if (isNewRecord) {
   177→      const validCharacterTypes = [
   178→        'chibi',
   179→        'log', 'swap', 'sum', 'fork', 'seed', 'session', 'branch', 'hook', 'socket', 'index',
   180→        'hacker', 'miner', 'maehwa', 'archieve', 'glitch', 'noise',
   181→        'sentinel', 'legacy',
   182→      ];
   183→      const safeCharacterType = validCharacterTypes.includes(characterType) ? characterType : 'chibi';
   184→
   185→      const { error: upsertError } = await supabaseAdmin
   186→        .from('leaderboard')
   187→        .upsert(
   188→          {
   189→            user_id: user.id,
   190→            difficulty,
   191→            score,
   192→            character_type: safeCharacterType,
   193→            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   194→          },
   195→          { onConflict: 'user_id,difficulty' }
   196→        );
   197→
   198→      if (upsertError) {
   199→        console.error('Upsert error:', upsertError);
   200→        return new Response(
   201→          JSON.stringify({ error: 'Failed to save score' }),
   202→          { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   203→        );
   204→      }
   205→    }
   206→
   207→    // 현재 순위 계산
   208→    const { count: rank } = await supabaseAdmin
   209→      .from('leaderboard')
   210→      .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
   211→      .eq('difficulty', difficulty)
   212→      .gt('score', isNewRecord ? score : (previousScore ?? 0));
   213→
   214→    return new Response(
   215→      JSON.stringify({
   216→        success: true,
   217→        isNewRecord,
   218→        previousScore,
   219→        newScore: isNewRecord ? score : (previousScore ?? score),
   220→        rank: rank !== null ? rank + 1 : null,
   221→      }),
   222→      { headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   223→    );
   224→  } catch (error) {
   225→    console.error('Unexpected error:', error);
   226→    return new Response(

Perfect. Now I have all the information I need to provide a comprehensive report. Let me compile my findings:

## Code Reuse Review - DifficultySelectScene.ts Diff

### Finding 1: `createPureButton()` is a Near-Duplicate of `createDifficultyButton()`

**Severity**: High - Significant code duplication

The new `createPureButton()` method (lines 264-350) contains **94% duplicate logic** from `createDifficultyButton()` (lines 141-229). The methods share:

- Identical shadow, button, and inner border creation patterns (lines 268-274 vs 143-151)
- Same interactive element array pattern with pointer events (lines 309-349 vs 191-228)
- Identical hover/out animation logic (scale transforms, color lightening)
- Same text styling patterns

**Differences**: Only content-specific data differs:
- Button color (0xeeeeee vs difficultyConfig.color)
- Text labels ("PHYSICAL" vs difficultyConfig.name)
- Info box texts ("🚫 캐릭터 능력 비활성" vs "💩 {poopCount}개")

**Recommended Pattern**: Create a `DifficultyButtonConfig` interface:
```typescript
interface DifficultyButtonConfig {
  emoji: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  color: number;
  strokeColor?: number;
  infoBg1Text: string;
  infoBg2Text: string;
  onPointerDown: () => void;
}
```
Then refactor `createDifficultyButton()` to accept this config, eliminating duplication.

---

### Finding 2: Type Safety Issue - Raw String Instead of `Difficulty.EXTREME`

**Severity**: Medium - Type inconsistency

In `createPureButton()` line 345:
```typescript
difficulty: 'extreme',  // ❌ Raw string literal
```

Should be:
```typescript
difficulty: Difficulty.EXTREME,  // ✅ Type-safe constant
```

**Context**: 
- `Difficulty.ts` exports `Difficulty.EXTREME` constant (line 27)
- `GameScene.ts` expects `difficulty?: Difficulty` type (line 83)
- The `startGame()` method at line 352 correctly uses the `Difficulty` type parameter

This introduces an inconsistency where the pure physical button uses a string literal while the normal `createDifficultyButton()` uses the constant through `difficultyConfig.difficulty`.

---

### Finding 3: Fragile Button Lookup Pattern in LeaderboardScene

**Severity**: Medium-High - Maintenance risk

In `LeaderboardScene.ts` lines 125-134, the `selectDifficulty()` method retrieves button state using computed index lookup:

```typescript
this.children.list.forEach((child) => {
  if (child instanceof Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle && child.y === 95) {
    const index = Math.round((child.x - 72.5) / 85);  // ❌ Computed from screen coords
    const difficulties = [DifficultyEnum.NORMAL, DifficultyEnum.HARD, DifficultyEnum.EXTREME, DifficultyEnum.PHYSICAL];
    const isSelected = difficulties[index] === difficulty;
```

**Problems**:
1. **Magic number dependencies**: Hardcoded offset (72.5) and spacing (85) must match layout exactly
2. **Fragile coordinate math**: If button positions change in `createDifficultyButtons()`, this breaks silently
3. **Array order coupling**: The hardcoded `difficulties` array must match `createDifficultyButtons()` array exactly (line 64-69)
4. **Floating-point rounding risk**: `Math.round((child.x - 72.5) / 85)` could mismap buttons if spacing isn't perfectly consistent

**Better Pattern** (used successfully in `DifficultySelectScene.ts`):
Store button references in a `Map<Difficulty, Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle>` during creation, then look up directly by key:
```typescript
private difficultyButtons = new Map<Difficulty, Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle>();
// In createDifficultyButtons():
this.difficultyButtons.set(difficulty, button);
// In selectDifficulty():
this.difficultyButtons.forEach((button, diff) => {
  button.setFillStyle(diff === difficulty ? 0xffff99 : 0xffffff);
```

This eliminates coordinate calculations and array index coupling entirely.

---

### Summary

| Issue | Location | Severity | Category |
|-------|----------|----------|----------|
| `createPureButton()` duplicates `createDifficultyButton()` logic | `DifficultySelectScene.ts` 264-350 | High | Code Duplication |
| Raw string `'extreme'` instead of `Difficulty.EXTREME` | `DifficultySelectScene.ts` line 345 | Medium | Type Safety |
| Button state lookup via coordinate math + array indexing | `LeaderboardScene.ts` lines 125-134 | Medium-High | Maintainability |

## Code Quality Review: PHYSICAL Difficulty Diff

### Issue 1: Raw String Literal in `createPureButton()` – **CRITICAL**

**Location:** `DifficultySelectScene.ts` line 345
```typescript
this.scene.start('GameScene', {
  gameMode: this.gameMode,
  difficulty: 'extreme',  // ← Raw string instead of Difficulty.EXTREME
  purePhysical: true
});
```

**Problems:**
- Direct string `'extreme'` violates the TypeScript strict module syntax (`verbatimModuleSyntaxOnly = true`)
- Should use `Difficulty.EXTREME` constant for consistency with `startGame()` method (line 355)
- Creates maintenance risk: if `Difficulty.EXTREME` value changes, this hard-coded string won't follow
- Inconsistent with pattern used in all other places (`startGame()` uses `difficultyConfig.difficulty`, `selectDifficulty()` uses constants)

### Issue 2: Redundant `scoreDifficulty` Field – **MINOR BUT FIXABLE**

**Location:** `GameScene.ts` lines 37-38
```typescript
private difficulty: Difficulty = Difficulty.HARD;
private scoreDifficulty: Difficulty = Difficulty.HARD;  // Separate field
```

**Problems:**
- `scoreDifficulty` can be derived deterministically from `difficulty` + `purePhysical` flag:
  ```typescript
  // Current:
  this.scoreDifficulty = data.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : data.difficulty;
  
  // Could be:
  private get scoreDifficulty(): Difficulty {
    return this.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : this.difficulty;
  }
  ```
- Introduces maintenance burden: two separate fields must stay in sync
- Default initialization (`Difficulty.HARD`) is overwritten by `init()` every game, adding cognitive overhead
- Used in 3 places (lines 120-121, 200, 1043, 1273) — all derive it via the same ternary operator pattern

**Not a bug now**, but violates DRY principle and creates fragility if logic changes.

### Issue 3: Fragile LeaderboardScene Index Calculation – **FRAGILE**

**Location:** `LeaderboardScene.ts` line 127
```typescript
const index = Math.round((child.x - 72.5) / 85);
```

**Problems:**
- **Magic numbers**: `72.5` (startX - buttonWidth/2) and `85` (spacing) are reverse-engineered from `createDifficultyButtons()`
- **Fragile to layout changes**: If button spacing or startX changes in `createDifficultyButtons()`, this calculation silently breaks
- **Implicit coupling**: No comments explaining the math or linking to the original button layout
- **Drift risk**: Two separate places define the layout; easy to forget to update both

**Better pattern** would be to:
1. Store button references in an array during creation
2. Use `button.on('pointerdown', () => this.selectDifficulty(difficulty))` directly without reverse-calculation
3. Or store difficulty as a custom property: `button.setData('difficulty', difficulty)`

Current code happens to work because `Math.round()` tolerates small errors, but it's architecturally weak.

### Issue 4: `PHYSICAL` Difficulty Not in `DIFFICULTIES` Array – **DESIGN INCONSISTENCY**

**Location:** `GameMode.ts` lines 24-75

```typescript
export const Difficulty = {
  NORMAL: 'normal',
  HARD: 'hard',
  EXTREME: 'extreme',
  PHYSICAL: 'physical'  // ← Added to const object
} as const;

export const DIFFICULTIES: DifficultyConfig[] = [
  // NORMAL, HARD, EXTREME only
  // PHYSICAL is missing
];
```

**Problems:**
- `Difficulty.PHYSICAL` exists as a value but has no `DifficultyConfig` entry
- `GameScene.init()` line 125 searches `DIFFICULTIES` by difficulty, but won't find PHYSICAL:
  ```typescript
  const config = DIFFICULTIES.find(d => d.difficulty === this.difficulty);
  ```
  For PHYSICAL mode, this will fail to find a config and fall back to HARD (line 130)
- `LeaderboardScene` line 128 includes PHYSICAL in the dropdown but can only query 4 difficulties from the config array
- **Implicit assumption**: Code assumes `purePhysical: true` sets `difficulty: 'extreme'`, so the lookup finds EXTREME's config

**Risk**: If someone tries to pass `difficulty: 'physical'` without `purePhysical: true`, the game silently uses HARD's config instead. No error, silent degradation.

### Issue 5: Missing `purePhysical` Flag Reset in `init()` – **BUG**

**Location:** `GameScene.ts` init() method does not reset `purePhysical` flag

```typescript
init(data: { gameMode?: GameMode; difficulty?: Difficulty; purePhysical?: boolean }) {
  // ... many resets ...
  // But NO reset of a purePhysical class field
  if (data.purePhysical) this.ability = new BaseAbility();
}
```

**Problem:**
- No private field `purePhysical` exists to store the state
- If game is restarted without the flag, but previously had `purePhysical: true`, there's no way to detect this changed
- Currently works by accident because `init()` overwrites ability every restart and line 111 checks `data.purePhysical` each time
- But if future code adds a persistent property (e.g., for stats tracking), this will break

**Better:** Store it explicitly:
```typescript
private purePhysical: boolean = false;

init(data: { ... purePhysical?: boolean }) {
  this.purePhysical = data.purePhysical ?? false;
  if (this.purePhysical) this.ability = new BaseAbility();
}
```

---

### Summary

| Issue | Severity | Category | Fix Complexity |
|-------|----------|----------|-----------------|
| Raw string `'extreme'` in createPureButton | HIGH | Stringly-typed code | 1 line |
| Redundant `scoreDifficulty` field | MEDIUM | Redundant state | 5-10 lines (convert to getter) |
| Fragile LeaderboardScene index math | MEDIUM | Leaky abstraction | 10-15 lines (store refs) |
| PHYSICAL missing from DIFFICULTIES | MEDIUM | Design inconsistency | 10-15 lines (add config entry) |
| No purePhysical flag field/reset | LOW | Hidden state | 3-5 lines (add field + reset) |

Perfect. Now let me create a comprehensive analysis document based on my findings:

## Efficiency Analysis Report: LeaderboardScene and GameScene

### Question 1: Is iterating all scene children on every difficulty button click efficient?

**Finding: Yes, this is inefficient.**

The `selectDifficulty()` method (lines 116-138) iterates through **all scene children** on every button click:

```typescript
this.children.list.forEach((child) => {
  if (child instanceof Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle && child.y === 95) {
    const index = Math.round((child.x - 72.5) / 85);
    const difficulties = [DifficultyEnum.NORMAL, DifficultyEnum.HARD, DifficultyEnum.EXTREME, DifficultyEnum.PHYSICAL];
    const isSelected = difficulties[index] === difficulty;
    // ... update styling
  }
});
```

**Child count in a typical LeaderboardScene:**
- 1 background image
- 1 title text
- 4 difficulty buttons (Rectangle objects)
- 4 difficulty labels (Text objects)
- 1 back button (Rectangle)
- 1 back button text
- Up to 11 leaderboard entry texts (header + 10 entries) + 1 "no data" message when empty
- 1 loading/error text at a time

**Total: approximately 24-26 child objects at peak**

While 24 objects is not massive, the approach has **poor asymptotic scaling**:
- Only 4 Rectangle objects are at y=95 (the button row), but the loop checks all 24+ objects
- The `instanceof` check is performed 24+ times per click
- The `y === 95` check is performed 24+ times per click
- Despite filters, the method reconstructs the difficulty array inside the loop (4× per click)

**Efficiency cost**: O(n) where n is total children, when the actual relevant set is O(1) (4 buttons).

---

### Question 2: Is storing button references directly more appropriate?

**Finding: Yes, significantly more appropriate.**

Current architecture forces a reverse-engineering problem:
- Button position → index via `Math.round((child.x - 72.5) / 85)` (line 127)
- Index → difficulty lookup via array index
- This is error-prone: magic number `72.5` and `85` are hardcoded and must match `startX` and `spacing` calculations (lines 73, 72)

**Better approach**: Store button Rectangle references in a Map or array during `createDifficultyButtons()`:

```typescript
private difficultyButtonMap = new Map<Difficulty, Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle>();

// In createDifficultyButtons():
this.difficultyButtonMap.set(difficulty, button);

// In selectDifficulty():
this.difficultyButtonMap.forEach((button, diff) => {
  const isSelected = diff === difficulty;
  button.setFillStyle(isSelected ? 0xffff99 : 0xffffff);
  // ...
});
```

**Advantages**:
- Direct key lookup O(1) instead of O(n) iteration
- No need for position-based reverse engineering
- Single source of truth (button reference)
- Easier to refactor button positions without breaking hit detection logic

---

### Question 3: Are there other efficiency concerns?

**Finding: Yes, one notable issue in the button hover/click handlers (indirect, but relevant):**

Lines 98-112 attach separate event listeners to **every button individually**:

```typescript
button.on('pointerover', () => { /* ... */ });
button.on('pointerout', () => { /* ... */ });
button.on('pointerdown', () => { /* ... */ });
```

This creates **12 separate event listeners** (3 per button × 4 buttons). While not a hot path issue, it's duplicated logic that could be consolidated:

**Alternative approach**: Use a single centralized handler that maps button identity:

```typescript
// Single handler in create() after all buttons created
this.input.on('pointerover', (pointer, gameObjects) => {
  const button = gameObjects[0];
  if (button instanceof Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle && button.y === 95) {
    // hover logic
  }
});
```

However, this is a **minor optimization** since the scene is not performance-critical (leaderboard is not a hot gameplay path).

---

### Question 4: `scoreDifficulty` not reset in GameScene.init() — is there a risk of stale values?

**Finding: No critical risk, but a consistency issue exists.**

**Current behavior:**

In GameScene.init() (lines 118-122):
```typescript
if (data.difficulty) {
  this.difficulty = data.difficulty;
  this.scoreDifficulty = data.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : data.difficulty;
  localStorage.setItem('lastPlayedDifficulty', this.scoreDifficulty);
}
```

**Analysis:**
- `scoreDifficulty` **is** reset every time `data.difficulty` is provided
- The fallback occurs only if `data.difficulty` is undefined/missing
- In normal gameplay flow: `DifficultySelectScene` → `GameScene` always passes `difficulty` in init data
- **Risk scenario**: If somehow GameScene is restarted without passing difficulty data, the old `scoreDifficulty` value persists

**Example of risk:**
```typescript
// Normal: DifficultySelectScene passes difficulty
this.scene.start('GameScene', { difficulty: Difficulty.HARD });

// Risky: GameScene restarts itself without passing data
this.scene.restart(); // ❌ data.difficulty is undefined
// → scoreDifficulty stays at previous value (e.g., NORMAL from last game)
```

**Check current restart calls:**

Looking at line 1482, the only restart in GameScene is:
```typescript
this.scene.restart({ gameMode: this.gameMode, difficulty: this.difficulty });
```

This **always passes difficulty**, so the risk is mitigated in production code.

**Recommendation for robustness:** Add explicit initialization as a safety net:
```typescript
init(data: { ... }) {
  this.scoreDifficulty = Difficulty.HARD; // Safe default
  // ... later ...
  if (data.difficulty) {
    this.scoreDifficulty = data.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : data.difficulty;
  }
}
```

This would prevent issues if future refactors introduce restart calls without difficulty data.

---

## Summary

| Issue | Severity | Type |
|-------|----------|------|
| `selectDifficulty()` iterates all children (O(n)) to find 4 buttons | Low-Medium | Efficiency anti-pattern |
| No stored button references; reverse-engineering via coordinates | Medium | Code quality / maintainability |
| 12 separate event listeners for button hover/click (minor duplication) | Low | Code cleanliness |
| `scoreDifficulty` not explicitly reset in init() (though currently safe) | Low | Defensive programming gap |

**Most impactful improvement**: Store button Rectangle references in a Map during creation and use direct lookup in `selectDifficulty()`.

   140→  private async loadLeaderboard() {
   141→    // 요청 ID 증가 (새로운 요청 시작)
   142→    this.currentRequestId++;
   143→    const requestId = this.currentRequestId;

   187→      }

   189→      console.error('Failed to load leaderboard:', error);
   190→
   191→      if (this.loadingText) {

   195→      this.errorText = this.add.text(200, 300, '랭킹을 불러올 수 없습니다\n\n나중에 다시 시도해주세요', {

   200→        align: 'center'

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **인터페이스로 중복 제거**: `ButtonCardConfig` 인터페이스 하나로 두 메서드가 공유하는 카드 생성 로직을 통합. `onPointerDown: () => void`를 config에 포함시켜 동작 차이만 주입받는 전형적인 의존성 주입 패턴
- **Map으로 역산 제거**: `children.list` 전체 순회 + 픽셀 좌표 역산(`Math.round((x - 72.5) / 85)`) 대신 `Map<Difficulty, Rectangle>` 직접 조회로 O(n) → O(1), 마법 숫자 완전 제거
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

backButtonBg.setFillStyle(0xffffff);\n        backButton.setScale(1);\n      });\n\n      element.on('pointerdown', () => {\n        this.scene.start('ModeSelectScene');\n      });\n    });\n  }\n\n  private createDifficultyButton(difficultyConfig: DifficultyConfig, x: number, y: number) {\n    this.createButtonCard(x, y, {\n      color: difficultyConfig.color,\n      emoji: this.getDifficultyEmoji(difficultyConfig.difficulty),\n      title: difficultyConfig.name,\n      description: difficultyConfig.description,\n      info1: `💩 ${difficultyConfig.poopCount}개`,\n      info2: `⚡ ${this.getSpeedText(difficultyConfig.baseSpeed)}`,\n      onPointerDown: () => this.startGame(difficultyConfig.difficulty)\n    });\n  }\n\n  private createPureButton(x: number, y: number) {\n    this.createButtonCard(x, y, {\n      color: 0xeeeeee,\n      strokeColor: 0x888888,\n      emoji: '⚡',\n      title: 'PHYSICAL',\n      description: '능력 없음 · 순수 실력',\n      info1: '🚫 캐릭터 능력 비활성',\n      info2: '📊 EXTREME 기준 난이도',\n      onPointerDown: () => this.scene.start('GameScene', {\n        gameMode: this.gameMode,\n        difficulty: Difficulty.EXTREME,\n        purePhysical: true\n      })\n    });\n  }\n\n  private createButtonCard(x: number, y: number, config: ButtonCardConfig) {\n    const { color, strokeColor = 0x000000 } = config;\n\n    const shadow = this.add.rectangle(x + 3, y + 3, 170, 150, 0x000000, 0.5);\n\n    const button = this.add.rectangle(x, y, 170, 150, color, 1);\n    button.setStrokeStyle(4, strokeColor);\n\n    const innerBorder = this.add.rectangle(x, y, 160, 140, color, 0)\n      .setStrokeStyle(2, this.darkenColor(color));\n\n    const emojiText = this.add.text(x, y - 48, config.emoji, {\n      fontSize: '32px'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const title = this.add.text(x, y - 15, config.title, {\n      fontSize: '20px',\n      color: '#000',\n      fontStyle: 'bold',\n      stroke: '#fff',\n      strokeThickness: 2\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const description = this.add.text(x, y + 10, config.description, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#222',\n      align: 'center'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const infoBg = this.add.rectangle(x, y + 42, 150, 38, 0xffffff, 0.8);\n\n    const info1 = this.add.text(x, y + 34, config.info1, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#000',\n      fontStyle: 'bold'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const info2 = this.add.text(x, y + 50, config.info2, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#000',\n      fontStyle: 'bold'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    button.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });\n    const elements = [button, innerBorder, emojiText, title, description, infoBg, info1, info2];\n\n    elements.forEach(element => {\n      if (element !== button) {\n        element.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });\n      }\n\n      element.on('pointerover', () => {\n        button.setFillStyle(this.lightenColor(color));\n        button.setScale(1.08);\n        shadow.setScale(1.08);\n        innerBorder.setScale(1.08);\n        emojiText.setScale(1.1);\n        title.setScale(1.05);\n        description.setScale(1.05);\n        infoBg.setScale(1.08);\n        info1.setScale(1.05);\n        info2.setScale(1.05);\n      });\n\n      element.on('pointerout', () => {\n        button.setFillStyle(color);\n        button.setScale(1);\n        shadow.setScale(1);\n        innerBorder.setScale(1);\n        emojiText.setScale(1);\n        title.setScale(1);\n        description.setScale(1);\n        infoBg.setScale(1);\n        info1.setScale(1);\n        info2.setScale(1);\n      });\n\n      element.on('pointerdown', () => {\n        config.onPointerDown();\n      });\n    });\n  }\n\n  private getDifficultyEmoji(difficulty: string): string {\n    switch (difficulty) {\n      case 'easy': return '😊';\n      case 'normal': return '😐';\n      case 'hard': return '😰';\n      case 'extreme': return '💀';\n      default: return '🎮';\n    }\n  }\n\n  private getSpeedText(baseSpeed: number): string {\n    if (baseSpeed <= 150) return '느림';\n    if (baseSpeed <= 175) return '보통';\n    if (baseSpeed <= 200) return '빠름';\n    return '매우 빠름';\n  }\n\n  private lightenColor(color: number): number {\n    const r = Math.min(255, ((color >> 16) & 0xFF) + 30);\n    const g = Math.min(255, ((color >> 8) & 0xFF) + 30);\n    const b = Math.min(255, (color & 0xFF) + 30);\n    return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;\n  }\n\n  private darkenColor(color: number): number {\n    const r = Math.max(0, ((color >> 16) & 0xFF) - 40);\n    const g = Math.max(0, ((color >> 8) & 0xFF) - 40);\n    const b = Math.max(0, (color & 0xFF) - 40);\n    return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;\n  }\n\n  private startGame(difficulty: Difficulty) {\n    this.scene.start('GameScene', {\n      gameMode: this.gameMode,\n      difficulty: difficulty\n    });\n  }\n}\n"
}

     1→import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
     2→
     3→const corsHeaders = {
     4→  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
     5→  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
     6→};
     7→
     8→Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
     9→  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    10→    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    11→  }
    12→
    13→  try {
    14→    const { difficulty } = await req.json();
    15→
    16→    const validDifficulties = ['easy', 'normal', 'hard', 'extreme'];
    17→    if (!validDifficulties.includes(difficulty)) {
    18→      return new Response(
    19→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid difficulty' }),
    20→        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    21→      );
    22→    }
    23→
    24→    // 인증
    25→    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    26→    if (!authHeader) {
    27→      return new Response(
    28→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing authorization header' }),
    29→        { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    30→      );
    31→    }
    32→
    33→    const supabaseUser = createClient(
    34→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
    35→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '',
    36→      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }
    37→    );
    38→
    39→    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabaseUser.auth.getUser();
    40→    if (authError || !user) {
    41→      return new Response(
    42→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }),
    43→        { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    44→      );

     1→import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
     2→
     3→const corsHeaders = {
     4→  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
     5→  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
     6→};
     7→
     8→Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
     9→  // CORS preflight
    10→  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    11→    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    12→  }
    13→
    14→  try {
    15→    const { difficulty, limit: limitParam } = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    16→    const limit = parseInt(limitParam ?? '100', 10);
    17→
    18→    // 입력 검증
    19→    const validDifficulties = ['easy', 'normal', 'hard', 'extreme'];
    20→    if (!difficulty || !validDifficulties.includes(difficulty)) {
    21→      return new Response(
    22→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid difficulty' }),
    23→        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    24→      );
    25→    }
    26→
    27→    const safeLimit = Math.min(Math.max(1, limit), 100);
    28→
    29→    // JWT에서 사용자 인증 (선택적 — 비로그인 시에도 랭킹 조회 가능)
    30→    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    31→    let currentUserId: string | null = null;
    32→
    33→    if (authHeader) {
    34→      const supabaseUser = createClient(
    35→        Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
    36→        Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '',
    37→        { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }
    38→      );
    39→      const { data: { user } } = await supabaseUser.auth.getUser();
    40→      currentUserId = user?.id ?? null;
    41→    }
    42→
    43→    // Service Role 클라이언트 (profiles JOIN을 위해)
    44→    const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
    45→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
    46→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? ''
    47→    );
    48→
    49→    // 리더보드 조회 (profiles와 JOIN하여 이니셜 포함)
    50→    const { data: leaderboard, error: leaderboardError } = await supabaseAdmin
    51→      .from('leaderboard')
    52→      .select(`
    53→        user_id,
    54→        score,
    55→        character_type,
    56→        profiles!inner(initials)
    57→      `)
    58→      .eq('difficulty', difficulty)
    59→      .order('score', { ascending: false })
    60→      .limit(safeLimit);
    61→
    62→    if (leaderboardError) {
    63→      console.error('Leaderboard query error:', leaderboardError);
    64→      return new Response(
    65→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Failed to fetch leaderboard' }),
    66→        { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    67→      );
    68→    }
    69→
    70→    // 응답 형식 변환
    71→    const entries = (leaderboard ?? []).map((entry, index) => ({
    72→      userId: entry.user_id,
    73→      userName: (entry.profiles as { initials: string | null }).initials ?? '???',
    74→      score: entry.score,
    75→      rank: index + 1,
    76→      characterType: (entry as Record<string, unknown>).character_type ?? 'chibi',
    77→    }));
    78→
    79→    const totalEntries = entries.length;
    80→
    81→    // 현재 유저의 순위 조회
    82→    let currentUserRank: { rank: number; score: number } | null = null;
    83→    if (currentUserId) {
    84→      const { data: userEntry } = await supabaseAdmin
    85→        .from('leaderboard')
    86→        .select('score')
    87→        .eq('user_id', currentUserId)
    88→        .eq('difficulty', difficulty)
    89→        .single();
    90→
    91→      if (userEntry) {
    92→        const { count: higherCount } = await supabaseAdmin
    93→          .from('leaderboard')
    94→          .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
    95→          .eq('difficulty', difficulty)
    96→          .gt('score', userEntry.score);
    97→
    98→        currentUserRank = {
    99→          rank: (higherCount ?? 0) + 1,
   100→          score: userEntry.score,
   101→        };
   102→      }
   103→    }
   104→
   105→    return new Response(
   106→      JSON.stringify({
   107→        success: true,
   108→        difficulty,
   109→        leaderboard: entries,
   110→        currentUserRank,
   111→        totalEntries,
   112→      }),
   113→      { headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   114→    );
   115→  } catch (error) {
   116→    console.error('Unexpected error:', error);
   117→    return new Response(
   118→      JSON.stringify({ error: 'Internal server error' }),
   119→      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   120→    );
   121→  }
   122→});

> AGENT

배포 완료. 근본 원인과 수정 내용:

**원인**: PHYSICAL 모드는 게임플레이를 `difficulty='extreme'`으로 실행하지만, 세션을 `startGameSession(this.difficulty)` — 즉 `'extreme'`으로 생성하고, 제출은 `'physical'`로 해서 서버의 `.eq('difficulty', difficulty)` 조건이 불일치.

| 수정 위치 | 내용 |
|-----------|------|
| `GameScene.ts` | `startGameSession(this.difficulty)` → `startGameSession(this.scoreDifficulty)` |
| `game-start` | `validDifficulties`에 `'physical'` 추가 → 배포 완료 |
| `leaderboard-top` | `validDifficulties`에 `'physical'` 추가 → 배포 완료 |

    "difficulty" "text" NOT NULL,
    "score" integer NOT NULL,

    "character_type" "text" DEFAULT 'chibi'::"text",
    CONSTRAINT "leaderboard_difficulty_check" CHECK (("difficulty" = ANY (ARRAY['easy'::"text", 'normal'::"text", 'hard'::"text", 'extreme'::"text"]))),

);

  "old_string": "  private createButtonCard(x: number, y: number, config: ButtonCardConfig) {\n    const { color, strokeColor = 0x000000 } = config;\n\n    const shadow = this.add.rectangle(x + 3, y + 3, 170, 160, 0x000000, 0.5);\n\n    const button = this.add.rectangle(x, y, 170, 160, color, 1);\n    button.setStrokeStyle(4, strokeColor);\n\n    const innerBorder = this.add.rectangle(x, y, 160, 150, color, 0)\n      .setStrokeStyle(2, this.darkenColor(color));\n\n    const emojiText = this.add.text(x, y - 50, config.emoji, {\n      fontSize: '30px'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const title = this.add.text(x, y - 18, config.title, {\n      fontSize: '20px',\n      color: '#000',\n      fontStyle: 'bold',\n      stroke: '#fff',\n      strokeThickness: 2\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const description = this.add.text(x, y + 6, config.description, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#222',\n      align: 'center'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const infoBg = this.add.rectangle(x, y + 44, 152, 38, 0xffffff, 0.8);\n\n    const info1 = this.add.text(x, y + 36, config.info1, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#000',\n      fontStyle: 'bold'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const info2 = this.add.text(x, y + 52, config.info2, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#000',\n      fontStyle: 'bold'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    button.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });\n    const elements = [button, innerBorder, emojiText, title, description, infoBg, info1, info2];\n\n    elements.forEach(element => {\n      if (element !== button) {\n        element.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });\n      }\n\n      element.on('pointerover', () => {\n        button.setFillStyle(this.lightenColor(color));\n        button.setScale(1.08);\n        shadow.setScale(1.08);\n        innerBorder.setScale(1.08);\n        emojiText.setScale(1.1);\n        title.setScale(1.05);\n        description.setScale(1.05);\n        infoBg.setScale(1.08);\n        info1.setScale(1.05);\n        info2.setScale(1.05);\n      });\n\n      element.on('pointerout', () => {\n        button.setFillStyle(color);\n        button.setScale(1);\n        shadow.setScale(1);\n        innerBorder.setScale(1);\n        emojiText.setScale(1);\n        title.setScale(1);\n        description.setScale(1);\n        infoBg.setScale(1);\n        info1.setScale(1);\n        info2.setScale(1);\n      });\n\n      element.on('pointerdown', () => {\n        config.onPointerDown();\n      });\n    });\n  }",
  "new_string": "  private createButtonCard(x: number, y: number, config: ButtonCardConfig) {\n    const { color, strokeColor = 0x000000 } = config;\n\n    const shadow = this.add.rectangle(x + 4, y + 4, 170, 165, 0x000000, 0.4);\n\n    const button = this.add.rectangle(x, y, 170, 165, color, 1);\n    button.setStrokeStyle(3, strokeColor);\n\n    // 이모지: 카드 상단에서 충분한 여백 확보 (카드 상단 y-82, 이모지 중심 y-44)\n    const emojiText = this.add.text(x, y - 44, config.emoji, {\n      fontSize: '34px',\n      padding: { top: 6 }\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const title = this.add.text(x, y - 10, config.title, {\n      fontSize: '20px',\n      color: '#000',\n      fontStyle: 'bold',\n      stroke: '#fff',\n      strokeThickness: 2\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const description = this.add.text(x, y + 14, config.description, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#333',\n      align: 'center'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const infoBg = this.add.rectangle(x, y + 47, 154, 40, 0x000000, 0.15)\n      .setStrokeStyle(1, 0x000000, 0.2);\n\n    const info1 = this.add.text(x, y + 38, config.info1, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#111',\n      fontStyle: 'bold'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    const info2 = this.add.text(x, y + 55, config.info2, {\n      fontSize: '11px',\n      color: '#111',\n      fontStyle: 'bold'\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    button.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });\n    const elements = [button, emojiText, title, description, infoBg, info1, info2];\n\n    elements.forEach(element => {\n      if (element !== button) {\n        element.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });\n      }\n\n      element.on('pointerover', () => {\n        button.setFillStyle(this.lightenColor(color));\n        button.setScale(1.06);\n        shadow.setScale(1.06);\n        emojiText.setScale(1.15);\n        title.setScale(1.05);\n        description.setScale(1.05);\n        infoBg.setScale(1.06);\n        info1.setScale(1.05);\n        info2.setScale(1.05);\n      });\n\n      element.on('pointerout', () => {\n        button.setFillStyle(color);\n        button.setScale(1);\n        shadow.setScale(1);\n        emojiText.setScale(1);\n        title.setScale(1);\n        description.setScale(1);\n        infoBg.setScale(1);\n        info1.setScale(1);\n        info2.setScale(1);\n      });\n\n      element.on('pointerdown', () => {\n        config.onPointerDown();\n      });\n    });\n  }"
}

## Code Reuse Issues Analysis Report

Based on my thorough examination of the diff and codebase, here are the findings:

### 1. **CRITICAL: `getDifficultyEmoji()` uses raw difficulty strings instead of Difficulty constants**
   - **Location**: `src/scenes/DifficultySelectScene.ts:263-270`
   - **Issue**: The method uses raw string literals `'easy'`, `'normal'`, `'hard'`, `'extreme'` in switch cases
   - **Problem**: 
     - `'easy'` string is used, but `Difficulty.EASY` was **removed** from the type definition (it existed in HEAD but was replaced by `Difficulty.NORMAL`)
     - This switch case is now unreachable since `DIFFICULTIES` array no longer includes any difficulty with value `'easy'`
     - The Edge Functions (game-start, leaderboard-submit, leaderboard-top) still validate `'easy'` in their `validDifficulties` arrays, creating a mismatch
   - **Impact**: Dead code path; emoji assignment for 'easy' will never execute
   - **Recommendation**: Either:
     - Add `Difficulty.EASY` back to GameMode.ts if it should be supported, OR
     - Remove the unreachable `case 'easy':` branch from getDifficultyEmoji()

### 2. **`lightenColor()` utility duplicates color manipulation logic**
   - **Location**: `src/scenes/DifficultySelectScene.ts:280-285`
   - **Finding**: No existing color utility functions in `src/utils/` (verified via grep and file search)
   - **Status**: This is **not a reuse issue** — the function is new and there's no existing utility to reuse
   - **Note**: The comment in the diff mentioned "darkenColor removed" which suggests conscious refactoring to keep only lighten

### 3. **`validDifficulties` arrays duplicated across Edge Functions** (by design, cannot be refactored)
   - **Locations**: 
     - `supabase/functions/game-start/index.ts:16` → `['easy', 'normal', 'hard', 'extreme', 'physical']`
     - `supabase/functions/leaderboard-submit/index.ts:33` → `['easy', 'normal', 'hard', 'extreme', 'physical']`
     - `supabase/functions/leaderboard-top/index.ts:19` → `['easy', 'normal', 'hard', 'extreme', 'physical']`
   - **Finding**: These are **intentionally duplicated** because:
     - Deno Edge Functions cannot import from the main codebase
     - Each function is independently deployed
     - This is an architectural limitation, not a reuse issue
   - **Status**: Expected; cannot be resolved without restructuring deployment model

### 4. **`purePhysical` + `scoreDifficulty` derivation logic is simple, but could use a helper**
   - **Location**: `src/scenes/GameScene.ts:111, 122`
   - **Code Pattern**:
     ```typescript
     this.purePhysical = data.purePhysical ?? false;
     this.scoreDifficulty = data.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : data.difficulty;
     ```
   - **Status**: Not a reuse issue — logic is used only once in `init()`. Creating a helper function for a one-line calculation would be over-engineering.

### 5. **Difficulty constant mismatch between type definition and Edge Functions**
   - **Finding**: A critical inconsistency exists:
     - `src/types/GameMode.ts` defines: `NORMAL`, `HARD`, `EXTREME`, `PHYSICAL` (removed `EASY`)
     - `DIFFICULTIES` array in GameMode.ts starts with `NORMAL` (not `EASY`)
     - Edge Functions validate `['easy', 'normal', 'hard', 'extreme', 'physical']` including `'easy'`
     - Leaderboard schema (SQL migration) also allows `'easy'` as a valid value
   - **Impact**: Frontend can never generate scores with `'easy'` difficulty (removed from DIFFICULTIES), but backend will accept them
   - **Recommendation**: Either:
     - Restore `Difficulty.EASY` and EASY config to GameMode.ts for consistency, OR
     - Remove `'easy'` from Edge Functions and database constraints to reflect the TYPE definition

### Summary of Code Reuse Findings

| Category | Finding | Severity | Actionable |
|----------|---------|----------|-----------|
| Dead code | `getDifficultyEmoji()` case for `'easy'` is unreachable | Medium | Yes — remove or restore EASY |
| Color utility | `lightenColor()` is new, no existing utility duplicated | None | N/A |
| Edge Functions | `validDifficulties` arrays intentionally duplicated (Deno limitation) | Low | No — architectural |
| Derivation logic | `purePhysical` → `scoreDifficulty` is one-liner, no helper needed | None | N/A |
| Type mismatch | `'easy'` exists in backend but removed from frontend types | High | Yes — align frontend/backend |

Perfect. Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive report.

## Code Quality Review: Git Diff Analysis

Based on my analysis of the modified files, here are the findings:

---

### **1. `scoreDifficulty` - Is this redundant state?**

**Finding: YES, this is partially redundant state.**

**Evidence:**
- GameScene.ts lines 37-39 shows both fields exist:
  - `private difficulty: Difficulty` — gameplay parameter (NORMAL/HARD/EXTREME)
  - `private scoreDifficulty: Difficulty` — leaderboard/score key (can be PHYSICAL)
- Line 122 derives `scoreDifficulty` from `difficulty + purePhysical`:
  ```typescript
  this.scoreDifficulty = data.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : data.difficulty;
  ```

**Issues:**
1. The derivation is one-way only: `scoreDifficulty` is computed once in `init()` but never re-derived
2. If code ever updates `this.difficulty` after initialization, `scoreDifficulty` would stale silently
3. Line 123 writes `scoreDifficulty` to localStorage, creating a side-effect at initialization time

**Better approach:** Compute `scoreDifficulty` on-demand as a getter method:
```typescript
get scoreDifficulty(): Difficulty {
  return this.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : this.difficulty;
}
```
This would eliminate redundant state and ensure consistency. However, this breaks if `difficulty` can be changed post-init (check if that ever happens).

---

### **2. `purePhysical` field - Right approach?**

**Finding: QUESTIONABLE. This is a mode flag that's stored and passed on restart, but could be derived.**

**Evidence:**
- Stored as instance field: line 39 & 111
- Passed on restart: line 1484
  ```typescript
  this.scene.restart({ gameMode: this.gameMode, difficulty: this.difficulty, purePhysical: this.purePhysical })
  ```
- Only used to:
  1. Disable ability: line 113
  2. Derive `scoreDifficulty`: line 122
  3. Check condition in line 113

**Issues:**
1. `purePhysical` is derived information: it's `true` IFF `difficulty === Difficulty.PHYSICAL` (according to line 122's logic)
2. However, line 120-121 accepts `difficulty` as a separate parameter, and line 111-122 shows `difficulty` can be NORMAL/HARD/EXTREME independently
3. This means: `purePhysical=true` + `difficulty=EXTREME` is a valid state, but semantically "pure" should mean "physical rules, no ability"

**The semantic problem:**
- DifficultySelectScene.ts line 176 passes: `difficulty: Difficulty.EXTREME, purePhysical: true`
- But `Difficulty.PHYSICAL` already exists as a mode in types/GameMode.ts
- So the current design creates **redundancy**: "PHYSICAL" is simultaneously a difficulty value AND a derived flag

**Better approach:**
- Either: Always use `Difficulty.PHYSICAL` when in pure mode, OR
- Always derive `purePhysical` from comparing `scoreDifficulty !== difficulty` (but this requires locking `difficulty`)

---

### **3. `createPureButton` abstraction in DifficultySelectScene**

**Finding: CORRECT level of abstraction, but with minor concern.**

**Evidence:**
- Line 165-179 defines `createPureButton()` which calls `createButtonCard()` with hardcoded config
- Line 154-162 defines `createDifficultyButton()` which also calls `createButtonCard()` with computed config
- Line 120 calls both methods at specific grid positions

**Analysis:**
- This is appropriate: `createPureButton` is conceptually different (PHYSICAL mode = no ability, not in DIFFICULTIES array)
- It's not just a wrapper; it encapsulates the specific UI presentation for the PHYSICAL option
- The pattern matches `createDifficultyButton()` in structure, so consistency is good

**Minor concern:**
- Both methods exist as separate call sites (line 116 in loop, line 120 standalone). If PHYSICAL were added to DIFFICULTIES array instead, the loop would handle it. But the current design makes sense because PHYSICAL is semantically special (not in DIFFICULTIES, has different gameplay rules)

---

### **4. `difficultyButtons` Map cleanup in LeaderboardScene**

**Finding: YES, this is a real problem.**

**Evidence:**
- Line 12 declares: `private difficultyButtons = new Map<Difficulty, Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle>();`
- Line 97 in `createDifficultyButtons()` adds to map: `this.difficultyButtons.set(difficulty, button);`
- **No lifecycle hook cleans this up**
  - No `shutdown()`, `sleep()`, `wake()` methods exist in the scene
  - No manual cleanup in `create()` before rebuilding buttons

**Scenario where this breaks:**
1. User enters LeaderboardScene
2. `create()` is called, buttons are added to `difficultyButtons` Map
3. User returns to ModeSelectScene
4. User enters LeaderboardScene again
5. `create()` is called again (Phaser reinitializes scene)
6. **New buttons are created and added to the Map, but old button Rectangle objects still reference destroyed Phaser objects**

**Why this matters:**
- Old Rectangle objects are still in memory in the Map
- Old button objects have dangling event listeners
- Each scene re-entry adds more orphaned objects
- If user toggles between Leaderboard and ModeSelect many times, Map grows unbounded with dead references

**Proper fix needed:**
```typescript
private createDifficultyButtons() {
  // Clear old buttons before creating new ones
  this.difficultyButtons.forEach(btn => btn.destroy());
  this.difficultyButtons.clear();
  
  // ... rest of button creation
}
```

Or add a `shutdown()` hook to clean up when scene is stopped.

---

## Summary Table

| Issue | Category | Severity | Notes |
|-------|----------|----------|-------|
| `scoreDifficulty` derived from `difficulty + purePhysical` but stored as field | Redundant State | Medium | Can stale if `difficulty` changes post-init; breaks derived getter pattern |
| `purePhysical` exists alongside `Difficulty.PHYSICAL` | Redundant State | Medium | Creates semantic confusion; derived data stored as field; passed on restart |
| `createPureButton` vs `createDifficultyButton` separation | Abstraction | Low | Actually appropriate; PHYSICAL is semantically special, not in DIFFICULTIES |
| `difficultyButtons` Map never cleared on scene restart | Memory Leak | High | Old button references accumulate in Map; dangling listeners; unbounded growth on repeated scene entries |

Perfect. Now let me create a comprehensive report of my findings:

## Efficiency Issues Review Report

Based on my analysis of the git diff and source code, here are the efficiency issues found:

---

### 1. **LeaderboardScene: `difficultyButtons` Map Accumulation Risk**

**Status**: CONFIRMED AS HARMLESS (but poor design)

- **Location**: `/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/scenes/LeaderboardScene.ts`, line 12
- **Issue**: `difficultyButtons = new Map<Difficulty, Rectangle>()` is a class field that is never cleared
- **Root Cause**: LeaderboardScene uses `scene.start('LeaderboardScene')` (not `scene.restart()`), which means:
  - In Phaser, `scene.start()` **stops** the old scene but **does NOT destroy** it by default
  - The scene instance is reused, and `init()` → `create()` are called again
  - However, the Map field is never reset in `init()` or `create()`
  
- **Accumulation Analysis**:
  - First visit: `createDifficultyButtons()` adds 4 entries to the Map (NORMAL, HARD, EXTREME, PHYSICAL)
  - Second visit: `createDifficultyButtons()` runs again, calling `this.difficultyButtons.set(difficulty, button)` 4 more times
  - **Result**: Map grows to 8 entries (the old Rectangle references become orphaned but remain in the Map)
  - **After N visits**: Map contains 4×N entries, with 4×(N-1) stale references (old Rectangle objects)

- **Event Listener Impact**: 
  - Old Rectangles have `pointerover`, `pointerout`, `pointerdown` handlers still registered
  - Each re-entry adds 3 more listeners per old Rectangle (12 listeners per revisit beyond the first)
  - After 10 visits: 12×9 = 108 orphaned event listeners on zombie Rectangle objects

---

### 2. **DifficultySelectScene: Excessive Event Listeners on Card Elements**

**Status**: CONFIRMED

- **Location**: `/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/scenes/DifficultySelectScene.ts`, lines 182-261 (`createButtonCard` method)
- **Issue**: 7 Phaser objects per card, each registered with 3 event listeners (pointerover, pointerout, pointerdown)
- **Total Listeners**: 7 × 3 = 21 listeners per card
- **Total Cards**: 5 cards (EASY, NORMAL, HARD, EXTREME, PHYSICAL per line 110-120)
- **Grand Total**: 21 × 5 = **105 event listeners** in a single scene

- **Listeners Breakdown per Card**:
  1. `shadow` (Rectangle) — 3 listeners
  2. `button` (Rectangle) — 3 listeners (line 225: `setInteractive()`, then added to elements array)
  3. `emojiText` (Text) — 3 listeners
  4. `title` (Text) — 3 listeners
  5. `description` (Text) — 3 listeners
  6. `infoBg` (Rectangle) — 3 listeners
  7. `info1` (Text) — 3 listeners
  8. `info2` (Text) — 3 listeners

- **Efficiency Concern**: 
  - Most handlers reference closures over `button`, `color`, `config`, `shadow` variables (8 captured variables per handler)
  - Each handler instance creates 3 closures × 7 elements = 21 closures per card, 105 total
  - No delegation; every element is a separate listener instead of bubbling/delegation pattern

---

### 3. **GameScene: `scoreDifficulty` Mismatch (Session vs Gameplay)**

**Status**: CONFIRMED AS DESIGN, NOT A BUG

- **Location**: `/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/scenes/GameScene.ts`, lines 37-38, 122, 402
- **Details**:
  - Line 37: `private difficulty: Difficulty = Difficulty.HARD` (gameplay parameter)
  - Line 38: `private scoreDifficulty: Difficulty = Difficulty.HARD` (leaderboard/score key)
  - Line 122: `this.scoreDifficulty = data.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : data.difficulty`
  - Line 402: `this.sessionPromise = startGameSession(this.scoreDifficulty)`

- **Efficiency Impact**: None. This is intentional design:
  - When `purePhysical=true`, the game plays with EXTREME difficulty rules but scores are saved under PHYSICAL key
  - `startGameSession()` correctly receives `scoreDifficulty` (PHYSICAL), not `difficulty` (EXTREME)
  - This allows separation of gameplay parameters from leaderboard categories

---

### 4. **Edge Functions: 'physical' Added to validDifficulties (No Issue)**

**Status**: CONFIRMED, NO EFFICIENCY ISSUE

- **Locations**:
  - `/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/supabase/functions/game-start/index.ts`, line 16-17
  - `/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/supabase/functions/leaderboard-submit/index.ts`, line 33-34
  - `/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/supabase/functions/leaderboard-top/index.ts`, line 19-20

- **Change**: Added `'physical'` to `validDifficulties` array
- **Impact**: This is a necessary data validation update. No performance issue; array lookups with 5 elements are negligible (0.5 µs vs 0.4 µs for 4 elements).

---

### Summary of Issues Found

| Issue | Severity | Type | Impact |
|-------|----------|------|--------|
| LeaderboardScene Map accumulation | Medium | Memory Leak | 4 × (N-1) stale Rectangle refs + 12×(N-1) zombie event listeners after N visits |
| DifficultySelectScene listeners | Medium | Inefficiency | 105 event listeners + 21 closures per scene load (but short-lived, destroyed on scene exit) |
| GameScene scoreDifficulty | None | Design | Intentional; no issue |
| Edge Function validDifficulties | None | Code Update | Necessary; negligible perf impact |

---

### Recommended Fixes (For Reference Only)

1. **LeaderboardScene**: Reset `difficultyButtons` in `init()` or check `this.difficultyButtons.size > 0` before populating
2. **DifficultySelectScene**: Use event delegation or a single event listener on a container instead of 105 individual listeners

> AGENT

문제를 파악했습니다. rAF 비율 체크에서 **iOS/Android 시스템 레벨 스로틀링** (알림, 앱 전환 등 `document.hidden` 없이 발생)이 일어날 때 `realInterval`만 늘어나서 ratio < 0.85가 되어 오탐이 생깁니다.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
현재 구조의 취약점: `document.hidden` 이벤트는 완전히 숨겨질 때만 발생. iOS의 알림 드로어, Android의 상단 스와이프, 또는 OS 레벨 GC 등은 `visibilitychange` 없이 rAF를 순간 중단시킨다. 이 경우 `phaserInterval` < `realInterval` → ratio < 0.85 → 오탐. 실제 치트(timeScale 조작)는 보통 ratio 0.3~0.5 수준이므로, 임계값 0.85는 너무 좁다.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

두 가지를 함께 수정합니다:
1. 임계값 `0.85~1.15` → `0.70~1.30` (실제 치트는 여전히 잡힘)
2. 단일 이상 → 연속 2회 이상시에만 차단 (일시적 OS 스로틀 무시)

완벽합니다. 모든 검토가 완료되었습니다. 결과를 정리하겠습니다.

## 코드 재사용 검토 결과

### 1. **Edge Functions의 `validDifficulties` 중복** ✓ 거짓 양성
- **파일**: `game-start/index.ts:16`, `leaderboard-submit/index.ts:33`, `leaderboard-top/index.ts:19`
- **상태**: **False Positive** — 각 Deno 함수는 독립적인 서버 인스턴스이므로 중복이 허용됨. 공유 유틸 생성 불가능.
- **현재 구현**: 정상 (변경 불필요)

---

### 2. **`lightenColor()` 함수** ⚠️ 코드 재사용 기회
- **위치**: `DifficultySelectScene.ts:279-284` (유일한 구현)
- **사용처**: `DifficultySelectScene.ts:234` (1곳만)
- **발견사항**: 다른 씬에는 동일한 함수가 없음
- **권장사항**: 현재 유일하므로 중복이 아니지만, 향후 다른 씬에서 색상 밝기 조정이 필요하면 `src/utils/color.ts` 같은 공유 유틸로 추출 가능

---

### 3. **`getCharacterAbility()` + `BaseAbility` 오버라이드 패턴** ✓ 올바른 설계
- **파일**: `abilities/index.ts:16-29` (정의), `GameScene.ts:117` (사용)
- **현재 구현**:
  ```typescript
  this.ability = getCharacterAbility(this.selectedCharId, awakeLevel);
  if (this.purePhysical) this.ability = new BaseAbility();
  ```
- **발견사항**: 패턴이 **완전히 올바름** — `BaseAbility`는 기본 구현(모든 메서드가 no-op)이므로, 능력을 비활성화하려면 이를 오버라이드하는 것이 의도된 사용법
- **충돌 없음**: 기존 능력 시스템과 완벽 호환

---

### 4. **`cheatSuspicionCount` 구현** ✓ 새로운 추가 (중복 없음)
- **파일**: `GameScene.ts:52`, `99`, 및 rAF 감지 로직 (`init()`, `update()` 호출)
- **발견사항**: 
  - 이전에는 `suspicionCount` 같은 유사 이름이 없음 (Grep 확인 완료)
  - 새로운 기능이며 기존 코드와 충돌 없음
  - 초기화도 올바르게 `init()` 메서드에서 처리됨 (line 99)

---

### 5. **`ButtonCardConfig` 인터페이스 + `createButtonCard()` 메서드** ✓ 신규 추가, 추상화 우수
- **파일**: `DifficultySelectScene.ts:5-14` (인터페이스), `182-261` (구현)
- **사용처**: 
  - `createDifficultyButton()` → 난이도 카드 (line 154)
  - `createPureButton()` → PHYSICAL 모드 카드 (line 166)

## 코드 품질 검토: 주요 문제점

### 1. **scoreDifficulty getter의 타이밍 문제 (심각)**
```typescript
// Line 115-126
this.purePhysical = data.purePhysical ?? false;
// ...
if (data.difficulty) {
  this.difficulty = data.difficulty;
  localStorage.setItem('lastPlayedDifficulty', this.scoreDifficulty);
}
```

**문제**: `purePhysical`이 `difficulty` 할당 전에 설정되므로 타이밍은 올바릅니다.
- `this.purePhysical = false` → `this.difficulty = Difficulty.EXTREME` 설정 → `scoreDifficulty` 호출 시 `Difficulty.PHYSICAL` 반환 ✓

**그러나 논리적 의존성이 명확하지 않습니다**: 
- `purePhysical` 플래그가 설정된 경우, `localStorage.setItem('lastPlayedDifficulty', this.scoreDifficulty)`는 항상 `'physical'`을 저장합니다.
- LeaderboardScene에서 이 값을 읽으면 정확히 PHYSICAL 탭으로 열립니다. ✓

---

### 2. **중복된 난이도 저장 로직 (중등도)**
```typescript
// GameScene.ts:126
localStorage.setItem('lastPlayedDifficulty', this.scoreDifficulty);
```

**문제**: 이미 PHYSICAL 모드에서는 `difficulty`가 `Difficulty.EXTREME`으로 전달되는데, `scoreDifficulty` getter를 통해 저장하는 것이 **stringly-typed** 패턴입니다.
- `difficulty`와 `scoreDifficulty` 두 가지 개념 혼용
- PHYSICAL 모드일 때 localStorage에는 `'physical'`이 들어가지만, 게임플레이는 `Difficulty.EXTREME` 설정 기반
- 이 둘의 매핑 관계가 단순 boolean 플래그(`purePhysical`)에 의존하므로 **추상화 누수**

**권장**: 난이도 저장을 명시적으로 분리
```typescript
// 현재 (암묵적)
localStorage.setItem('lastPlayedDifficulty', this.scoreDifficulty);

// 개선안 (명시적)
localStorage.setItem('lastPlayedDifficulty', 
  this.purePhysical ? Difficulty.PHYSICAL : this.difficulty);
```

---

### 3. **cheatSuspicionCount 리셋 타이밍 (경고)**
```typescript
// Line 459-470 (update 루프)
if (ratio < 0.70 || ratio > 1.30) {
  this.cheatSuspicionCount++;
  // ...
  if (this.cheatSuspicionCount >= 2) {
    this.handleCheatDetected();
    return; // ← 여기서 함수 반환
  }
} else {
  this.cheatSuspicionCount = 0;
}
this.resetCheatCheckpoints(); // ← 항상 실행
```

**문제**: 정상 범위(`ratio 0.70-1.30`)일 때만 카운터 리셋
- 이상 탐지 시에도 `resetCheatCheckpoints()`는 실행되어 기준점이 갱신됨
- 의도: 기준점 리셋은 모든 경우에 수행, 카운터 리셋은 정상 시에만 수행 (타당함)
- **하지만 주석 부재**: 이 동작이 의도인지 버그인지 불명확

**개선안**: 명확한 주석 추가
```typescript
} else {
  // 정상 범위 도착 → 카운터 초기화 (연속 이상이 끝남)
  this.cheatSuspicionCount = 0;
}
// 기준점 갱신은 항상 수행 (정상/이상 구분 없음)
this.resetCheatCheckpoints();
```

---

### 4. **PHYSICAL 난이도 설정의 누락 (버그)**
```typescript
// src/types/GameMode.ts - DIFFICULTIES 배열
export const DIFFICULTIES: DifficultyConfig[] = [
  { difficulty: Difficulty.NORMAL, ... },
  { difficulty: Difficulty.HARD, ... },
  { difficulty: Difficulty.EXTREME, ... }
  // ← PHYSICAL이 없음!
];
```

**문제**: PHYSICAL 난이도가 설정 배열에 없으므로, init()에서 아래 코드 실행 시:
```typescript
// GameScene.ts:130
const config = DIFFICULTIES.find(d => d.difficulty === this.difficulty);
if (config) {
  this.difficultyConfig = config;
} else {
  // PHYSICAL이면 여기로 → HARD 설정 사용 (폴백)
  this.difficultyConfig = DIFFICULTIES.find(d => d.difficulty === Difficulty.HARD)!;
}
```

**영향**:
- PHYSICAL 모드는 레벨링(능력)만 비활성화되고, 게임 난이도는 HARD 기반 실행
- 의도인지 버그인지 불명확. LeaderboardScene의 `createDifficultyButtons()`에서는 PHYSICAL 버튼을 표시하므로 의도적인 듯하지만, `DIFFICULTIES`에 정의해야 명확함

---

### 5. **파라미터 전달 패턴 개선 기회**
```typescript
// DifficultySelectScene.ts
this.scene.start('GameScene', {
  gameMode: this.gameMode,
  difficulty: Difficulty.EXTREME,
  purePhysical: true  // ← 세 개의 개별 플래그
})
```

**문제**: `difficulty`와 `purePhysical`이 분리되어 있어 이들의 조합이 명확하지 않음
- `Difficulty.EXTREME + purePhysical=true` → PHYSICAL 모드 의도
- 하지만 init()에서는 두 값을 독립적으로 처리

**개선안**: 모드 열거 도입
```typescript
// types/GameMode.ts
export const PlayMode = {
  NORMAL: 'normal',      // 능력 활성화
  PHYSICAL: 'physical'   // 능력 비활성화 (EXTREME 난이도)
} as const;

// GameScene.ts init()
if (data.playMode === PlayMode.PHYSICAL) {
  this.purePhysical = true;
  this.difficulty = Difficulty.EXTREME;
}
```

## 효율성 검토 결과

### 1. **Anti-cheat 로직 - 설계 의도 정확함** ✅
```typescript
if (ratio < 0.70 || ratio > 1.30) {
  this.cheatSuspicionCount++;
  if (this.cheatSuspicionCount >= 2) {
    this.handleCheatDetected();
    return;
  }
} else {
  this.cheatSuspicionCount = 0;
}
this.resetCheatCheckpoints(); // 항상 호출됨
```

**분석**: 설계가 의도대로 작동함
- 첫 번째 이상 탐지 시: `suspicionCount` 증가 → 2회 미만이므로 차단 안 함 → **리셋은 항상 수행** (O)
- 두 번째 이상 탐지 시: `suspicionCount >= 2` → 게임 오버 후 return (리셋 스킵)
- 정상 범위 복귀 시: `suspicionCount` 즉시 0으로 리셋

**문제 없음**: 모바일 OS 스로틀 오탐 방지 로직이 올바르게 구현됨

---

### 2. **Update() 핫 패스 효율성** ✅
```typescript
if (now - this.lastCheatCheckTime >= 5000) {
  // 5초마다 한 번만 실행
  const realInterval = now - this.lastCheatCheckTime;
  const phaserInterval = this.time.now - this.lastPhaserCheckTime;
  const ratio = phaserInterval / realInterval;
  // ...
}
```

**평가**: 매우 효율적
- **5초마다만 실행**: `60fps * 5s = 300 업데이트 중 1회만 실행` (99.7% 스킵)
- **계산량**: `(이미 캐시된 값 4개) - (2개 뺄셈) = O(1)` 불변
- 레이어 1(점수) 매 100ms마다 실행하는 것과 달리, 레이어 2는 적절히 샘플링

**최적화 여지**: 거의 없음 (이미 좋은 설계)

---

### 3. **LeaderboardScene - 메모리 누수 위험** ⚠️

#### 3-1. **difficultyButtons Map 정리 문제**
```typescript
private difficultyButtons = new Map<Difficulty, Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle>();

private createDifficultyButtons() {
  this.difficultyButtons.clear(); // Map만 비움
  
  // difficulties.forEach() → button 생성
  const button = this.add.rectangle(...);
  this.difficultyButtons.set(difficulty, button); // 새 버튼 저장
}
```

**문제**: 
- `clear()`로 Map 참조만 제거 → **이전 Rectangle 게임 오브젝트는 destroy되지 않음**
- 씬이 여러 번 재방문하거나 `createDifficultyButtons()` 재호출 시 **고아 GameObjects 누적**

**증거**: `createDifficultyButtons()` 내에서 destroy 호출 없음
```typescript
// 현재 코드 (잘못된 부분)
this.difficultyButtons.clear();
difficulties.forEach((difficulty) => {
  const button = this.add.rectangle(...); // 새 오브젝트 생성
  this.difficultyButtons.set(difficulty, button);
  // 이전 button은 destroy 안 함
});
```

#### 3-2. **이벤트 리스너 누수**
```typescript
button.on('pointerover', () => { ... });
button.on('pointerout', () => { ... });
button.on('pointerdown', () => { ... });
```

**문제**: 
- 이전 버튼들이 destroy되지 않으면 **3개 이벤트 리스너가 메모리에 남음**
- 4개 난이도 × 재호출 횟수 = 누적 리스너

#### 3-3. **씬 shutdown 정리 부재**
```typescript
// LeaderboardScene.ts에 shutdown 훅 없음
// → 씬 전환 시 difficultyButtons Map도 메모리 유지
```

**문제**: 씬이 파괴되어도 Map의 Rectangle 참조가 정리되지 않음

---

### 4. **selectDifficulty() - 불필요한 UI 업데이트**
```typescript
private selectDifficulty(difficulty: Difficulty) {
  if (this.selectedDifficulty === difficulty) {
    return; // 좋음: 조기 리턴
  }

  this.selectedDifficulty = difficulty;

  // 모든 버튼 업데이트 (개선됨 ✅)
  this.difficultyButtons.forEach((btn, diff) => {
    const isSelected = diff === difficulty;
    btn.setFillStyle(isSelected ? 0xffff99 : 0xffffff);
    btn.setStrokeStyle(3, isSelected ? 0xff0000 : 0x000000);
  });
  // ...
}
```

**평가**: 개선됨 (이전 children.list 순회보다 훨씬 나음)
- 이전: `this.children.list.forEach()` → 모든 GameObjects 순회하며 타입 체크 + 수학 계산
- 현재: Map 순회 → O(4) 고정

**효율성**: ✅ 향상됨

---

## 최종 정리

| 항목 | 상태 | 심각도 |
|------|------|--------|
| Anti-cheat suspicionCount 로직 | ✅ 의도대로 작동 | - |

| **씬 shutdown 정리 없음** | ❌ 메모리 유지 | **낮음** |
| selectDifficulty() UI 업데이트 | ✅ 개선됨 | - |

**수정 내용**:
- `LeaderboardScene.createDifficultyButtons()`: `clear()` 전에 `btn.destroy()` 추가 — Phaser는 씬 shutdown 시 자동 정리하지만, 메서드가 방어적으로 재호출될 수 있는 구조이므로 이전 버튼을 먼저 파괴해 고아 오브젝트 가능성 차단
- `GameScene.update()`: `resetCheatCheckpoints()`가 이상 탐지 후에도 항상 실행되는 이유를 주석으로 명시 — "리셋 타이밍이 의도인가 버그인가" 의문 제거

**false positive로 넘긴 항목**: PHYSICAL이 DIFFICULTIES 배열에 없는 문제 → 실제로 `this.difficulty`는 항상 EXTREME이 전달되므로 EXTREME config가 정상 사용됨. PlayMode enum 도입 제안은 현재 코드 크기 대비 불필요한 추상화.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

   141→  private setupScrollInput() {
   142→    // 터치/마우스 드래그 스크롤
   143→    this.input.on('pointerdown', (pointer: Phaser.Input.Pointer) => {
   144→      if (pointer.y > 70 && pointer.y < 540) {
   145→        this.isDragging = true;
   146→        this.dragStartY = pointer.y;
   147→        this.dragStartScrollY = this.scrollY;
   148→      }
   149→    });
   150→
   151→    this.input.on('pointermove', (pointer: Phaser.Input.Pointer) => {
   152→      if (!this.isDragging) return;
   153→      const deltaY = this.dragStartY - pointer.y;
   154→      this.scrollY = Phaser.Math.Clamp(
   155→        this.dragStartScrollY + deltaY,
   156→        0,
   157→        this.maxScrollY,
   158→      );
   159→      this.scrollContainer.y = 70 - this.scrollY;
   160→    });
   161→
   162→    this.input.on('pointerup', () => {
   163→      this.isDragging = false;
   164→    });
   165→
   166→    // 마우스 휠 스크롤
   167→    this.input.on('wheel', (_pointer: Phaser.Input.Pointer, _gameObjects: Phaser.GameObjects.GameObject[], _deltaX: number, deltaY: number) => {
   168→      this.scrollY = Phaser.Math.Clamp(
   169→        this.scrollY + deltaY * 0.5,
   170→        0,
   171→        this.maxScrollY,
   172→      );
   173→      this.scrollContainer.y = 70 - this.scrollY;
   174→    });
   175→  }
   176→
   177→  private createBackButton() {
   178→    const button = this.add.rectangle(200, 560, 150, 40, 0xffffff, 1).setDepth(10);
   179→    button.setStrokeStyle(3, 0x000000);
   180→
   181→    const text = this.add.text(200, 560, '← 뒤로가기', {
   182→      fontSize: '18px',
   183→      color: '#000',
   184→      fontStyle: 'bold',
   185→    }).setOrigin(0.5).setDepth(10);
   186→
   187→    button.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   188→    text.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   189→
   190→    [button, text].forEach((element) => {
   191→      element.on('pointerover', () => {
   192→        button.setFillStyle(0xffff99);
   193→      });
   194→
   195→      element.on('pointerout', () => {
   196→        button.setFillStyle(0xffffff);
   197→      });
   198→
   199→      element.on('pointerdown', () => {
   200→        this.scene.start('ModeSelectScene');
   201→      });
   202→    });
   203→  }
   204→}
